 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

      PLA-3     ▮▮▯  Third one in review                                    ◌ draft         1d ago

  ◔ In Progress 2  ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 ▎ ╭──────────────────────────────────╴ mode: key reference ╶───────────────────────────────────╮o
   │  keys  every key                                                                      esc  │o
   │  MOVE AROUND                                   CHANGE THE ISSUE                            │
  ○│   j / k  or  ↓ / ↑  next / previous             S                 status                   │─
   │   5j  /  5k         jump 5 rows                 P                 priority                 │o
   │   G                 last issue                  A                 assign                   │
  ◌│   type, or /        filter · esc clears         X                 cancel                   │─
   │   click / wheel     rows, hints · scroll        U                 undo last change         │o
   │                                                 n                 new issue                │
  ◈│  WORK ON THE ISSUE                                                                         │─
   │   enter  or  l      start working on it        VIEWS                                       │o
   │   space             preview                     b                 daily report             │
   │   w                 jump to its workspace       p                 to-do list               │
   │   a                 ask Col                     W                 active workspaces        │
   │   C                 comment                     t                 team                     │
   │   c                 free session with Col       T                 project                  │
   │                                                 R                 review inbox             │
   │  OPEN ELSEWHERE                                 r                 refresh                  │
   │   g                 pull request                h  or  esc        back, then close         │
   │   L                 Linear app                  q                 close                    │
   │   y                 copy link                   Z                 Col on / off             │
   │                                                 K                 footer hints on / off    │
   │                                                 D                 setup                    │
   │                                                 B                 give Col a bone          │
   │                                                 O                 the quick tour           │
   │                                                                                            │
   ╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯

   │                                                 R                 review inbox             │

   │   L                 Linear app                  q                 close                    │

  my issues   W active workspaces · p to-do · t team · T project · R re6 issues  ·  ✓ 1 done today
 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  ◕ In Review 1  ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      PLA-3     ▮▮▯  Third one in review                                    ◌ draft         1d ago

  ◔ In Progress 2  ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      PLA-1     !!!  First issue about login                                                1h ago
    ▶ PLA-2     ▮▮▮  Second issue with a workspace                 ▶ open   ● review        2d ago

  ○ Todo 1╭────────────────────────────╴ mode: guided tour ╶─────────────────────────────╮────────
      PLA-│  Col  a quick tour                                                      esc  │  1w ago
          │                                                                              │
  ◌ Backlo│   starting work, and the green markers                                       │────────
 ▎    PLA-│                                                                              │  3w ago
          │   When you start an issue with  l  /  enter , four things happen: it is      │
  ◈ Triage│   assigned to you and moved to In Progress, it gets a git worktree on its    │────────
      PLA-│   own branch, a herdr workspace named after it, and Claude Code opens        │  3d ago
          │   there with the issue already read.                                         │
          │                                                                              │
          │     ▶ my-repo                                                                │
          │   From then on its row carries this green marker: a workspace is open for    │
          │   it, work in progress, right now, on this machine. It names the             │
          │   workspace (its number in herdr's sidebar) and the repository.              │
          │                                                                              │
          │   From now on I open on just those rows;  W  switches to all your issues.    │
          │    w  on one jumps straight into it: each session you start is a tab         │
          │   there, and with several  w  asks which one.                                │
          │                                                                              │
          │   The loop:  open,  w  into what's in progress.  l  /  enter  starts         │
          │   something new.    b  in the morning,  p  for to-dos.    O : this tour.     │
          ╰─────────────────────────────  l   OK, got it! ───────────────────────────────╯
